  function niceInterval(range,target=15){
    const raw=Math.max(1,range/Math.max(4,target)),p=Math.pow(10,Math.floor(Math.log10(raw))),m=raw/p;
    return (m<=1?1:m<=2?2:m<=5?5:10)*p;

  function stitchContourSegments(segments){
    const used=new Uint8Array(segments.length),adj=new Map(),factor=100000;
    const key=p=>Math.round(p[0]*factor)+','+Math.round(p[1]*factor);
    function add(k,i){let a=adj.get(k);if(!a)adj.set(k,a=[]);a.push(i);}
    segments.forEach((s,i)=>{add(key(s[0]),i);add(key(s[1]),i);});
    const paths=[];
    function walk(startKey,startIndex){
      let currentKey=startKey,index=startIndex,guard=0,path=[];
      while(index!=null&&!used[index]&&guard++<segments.length+8){
        used[index]=1;const s=segments[index],forward=key(s[0])===currentKey,from=forward?s[0]:s[1],to=forward?s[1]:s[0];
        if(!path.length)path.push(from);path.push(to);currentKey=key(to);
        const next=(adj.get(currentKey)||[]).find(i=>!used[i]);index=next==null?null:next;
      }
      if(path.length>=4)paths.push(path);
    }
    for(const [k,list] of adj)if(list.length===1&&!used[list[0]])walk(k,list[0]);
    for(let i=0;i<segments.length;i++)if(!used[i])walk(key(segments[i][0]),i);
    return paths;
  }

  function niceInterval(range,target=15){
    const raw=Math.max(1,range/Math.max(4,target)),p=Math.pow(10,Math.floor(Math.log10(raw))),m=raw/p;
    return (m<=1?1:m<=2?2:m<=5?5:10)*p;
  }
  function stitchContourSegments(segments){
    const used=new Uint8Array(segments.length),adj=new Map(),factor=100000;
    const key=p=>Math.round(p[0]*factor)+','+Math.round(p[1]*factor);
    function add(k,i){let a=adj.get(k);if(!a)adj.set(k,a=[]);a.push(i);}
    segments.forEach((s,i)=>{add(key(s[0]),i);add(key(s[1]),i);});
    const paths=[];
    function walk(startKey,startIndex){
      let currentKey=startKey,index=startIndex,guard=0,path=[];
      while(index!=null&&!used[index]&&guard++<segments.length+8){
        used[index]=1;const s=segments[index],forward=key(s[0])===currentKey,from=forward?s[0]:s[1],to=forward?s[1]:s[0];
        if(!path.length)path.push(from);path.push(to);currentKey=key(to);
        const next=(adj.get(currentKey)||[]).find(i=>!used[i]);index=next==null?null:next;
      }
      if(path.length>=4)paths.push(path);
    }
    for(const [k,list] of adj)if(list.length===1&&!used[list[0]])walk(k,list[0]);
    for(let i=0;i<segments.length;i++)if(!used[i])walk(key(segments[i][0]),i);
    return paths;
  }

  function niceInterval(range,target=15){
    const raw=Math.max(1,range/Math.max(4,target)),p=Math.pow(10,Math.floor(Math.log10(raw))),m=raw/p;
    return (m<=1?1:m<=2?2:m<=5?5:10)*p;
  }
  function stitchContourSegments(segments){
    const used=new Uint8Array(segments.length),adj=new Map(),factor=100000;
    const key=p=>Math.round(p[0]*factor)+','+Math.round(p[1]*factor);
    function add(k,i){let a=adj.get(k);if(!a)adj.set(k,a=[]);a.push(i);}
    segments.forEach((s,i)=>{add(key(s[0]),i);add(key(s[1]),i);});
    const paths=[];
    function walk(startKey,startIndex){
      let currentKey=startKey,index=startIndex,guard=0,path=[];
      while(index!=null&&!used[index]&&guard++<segments.length+8){
        used[index]=1;const s=segments[index],forward=key(s[0])===currentKey,from=forward?s[0]:s[1],to=forward?s[1]:s[0];
        if(!path.length)path.push(from);path.push(to);currentKey=key(to);
        const next=(adj.get(currentKey)||[]).find(i=>!used[i]);index=next==null?null:next;
      }
      if(path.length>=4)paths.push(path);
    }
    for(const [k,list] of adj)if(list.length===1&&!used[list[0]])walk(k,list[0]);
    for(let i=0;i<segments.length;i++)if(!used[i])walk(key(segments[i][0]),i);
    return paths;
  }

  function niceInterval(range,target=15){
    const raw=Math.max(1,range/Math.max(4,target)),p=Math.pow(10,Math.floor(Math.log10(raw))),m=raw/p;
    return (m<=1?1:m<=2?2:m<=5?5:10)*p;

  function stitchContourSegments(segments){
    const used=new Uint8Array(segments.length),adj=new Map(),factor=100000;
    const key=p=>Math.round(p[0]*factor)+','+Math.round(p[1]*factor);
    function add(k,i){let a=adj.get(k);if(!a)adj.set(k,a=[]);a.push(i);}
    segments.forEach((s,i)=>{add(key(s[0]),i);add(key(s[1]),i);});
    const paths=[];
    function walk(startKey,startIndex){
      let currentKey=startKey,index=startIndex,guard=0,path=[];
      while(index!=null&&!used[index]&&guard++<segments.length+8){
        used[index]=1;const s=segments[index],forward=key(s[0])===currentKey,from=forward?s[0]:s[1],to=forward?s[1]:s[0];
        if(!path.length)path.push(from);path.push(to);currentKey=key(to);
        const next=(adj.get(currentKey)||[]).find(i=>!used[i]);index=next==null?null:next;
      }
      if(path.length>=4)paths.push(path);
    }
    for(const [k,list] of adj)if(list.length===1&&!used[list[0]])walk(k,list[0]);
    for(let i=0;i<segments.length;i++)if(!used[i])walk(key(segments[i][0]),i);
    return paths;
  }

        if((c<level)!=(d<level))pts.push([x+1-interp(c,d,level),y+1]);
        if((d<level)!=(a<level))pts.push([x,y+1-interp(d,a,level)]);

  function niceInterval(range,target=15){
    const raw=Math.max(1,range/Math.max(4,target)),p=Math.pow(10,Math.floor(Math.log10(raw))),m=raw/p;
    return (m<=1?1:m<=2?2:m<=5?5:10)*p;

  function stitchContourSegments(segments){
    const used=new Uint8Array(segments.length),adj=new Map(),factor=100000;
    const key=p=>Math.round(p[0]*factor)+','+Math.round(p[1]*factor);
    function add(k,i){let a=adj.get(k);if(!a)adj.set(k,a=[]);a.push(i);}
    segments.forEach((s,i)=>{add(key(s[0]),i);add(key(s[1]),i);});
    const paths=[];
    function walk(startKey,startIndex){
      let currentKey=startKey,index=startIndex,guard=0,path=[];
      while(index!=null&&!used[index]&&guard++<segments.length+8){
        used[index]=1;const s=segments[index],forward=key(s[0])===currentKey,from=forward?s[0]:s[1],to=forward?s[1]:s[0];
        if(!path.length)path.push(from);path.push(to);currentKey=key(to);
        const next=(adj.get(currentKey)||[]).find(i=>!used[i]);index=next==null?null:next;
      }
      if(path.length>=4)paths.push(path);
    }
    for(const [k,list] of adj)if(list.length===1&&!used[list[0]])walk(k,list[0]);
    for(let i=0;i<segments.length;i++)if(!used[i])walk(key(segments[i][0]),i);
    return paths;
  }
